Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a sorting algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 101. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 453/4.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on sorting structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 101. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 453. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any sorting module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 513. The projective resolution has length at most 404 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the sorting space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 513 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 404. QED.
